[✅] [2026-01-08 01:21:25 UTC] [Agent: Scribe Coder] [Project: scribe_systematic_audit_1] SPEC-SET-001 Implementation Summary:  **Bug Fixed**: Empty progress logs (after rotation) were incorrectly treated as NEW projects instead of EXISTING projects.  **Root Cause**: Original code used `entry_count == 0` to determine if a project was new, which couldn't distinguish between: - Genuinely NEW project (first time created) - EXISTING project with rotated/empty log  **Solution Implemented**: - Modified `shared/project_utils.py::detect_project_state()` to accept `docs_were_generated` parameter - Changed detection logic: If docs were just created in THIS call → NEW, otherwise → EXISTING_LEGACY - Updated `tools/set_project.py` to pass flag: `docs_were_generated = len(doc_result.get("files", [])) > 0`  **Tests Created**:  - `test_bug_001_empty_log_shows_existing_sitrep`: Verifies rotated logs show EXISTING SITREP ✓ - `test_bug_001_genuinely_new_project`: Ensures truly new projects still work ✓  **Files Modified**: 1. shared/project_utils.py (lines 11-97): Added docs_were_generated... | value={"implementation_time_minutes": 30, "spec": "SPEC-SET-001", "confidence": 0.95, "tests_passing": 2, "tests_created": 2, "lines_changed": 25, "reasoning": {"why": "Provide complete implementation summary for audit trail", "what": "Document all changes, rationale, and verification steps", "how": "Comprehensive summary with technical details and test results"}}}; priority=medium; log_type=progress; content_type=log
